Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
773 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
774 |     if (item == null) {
775 |       return null;
776 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
777 |       return item.next;
778 |     } else {
779 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
780 |       if (result != item.next) {
781 |         return new PropListItem(
782 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
783 |       } else {
784 |         return item;
785 |       }
786 |     }
787 |   }

242 |   private void addToString(int c) {
243 |     int N = stringBufferTop;
244 |     if (N == stringBuffer.length) {
245 |         char[] tmp = new char[stringBuffer.length * 2];
246 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, N);
247 |         stringBuffer = tmp;
248 |     }
249 |     stringBuffer[N] = (char)c;
250 |     stringBufferTop = N + 1;

Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1721-1754
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1721 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1722 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1723 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1724 |     }
1725 | 
1726 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1727 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1728 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1729 |       skipEOLs();
1730 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1731 |         next();
1732 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1733 |       }
1734 |     }
1735 | 
1736 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1737 | 
1738 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1739 |       next();
1740 |       skipEOLs();
1741 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1742 |       if (memberType != null) {
1743 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1744 | 
1745 |         skipEOLs();
1746 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1747 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1748 |         }
1749 | 
1750 |         next();
1751 |       }
1752 |     }
1753 |     return typeNameNode;
1754 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether the given name is visible in the current context.
   * @param t The current traversal.
   * @param name The name node.
   */
Method code:
323 |   private void checkNameVisibility(NodeTraversal t, Node name, Node parent) {
324 |     Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
325 |     if (var != null) {
326 |       JSDocInfo docInfo = var.getJSDocInfo();
327 |       if (docInfo != null) {
328 |         // If a name is private, make sure that we're in the same file.
329 |         Visibility visibility = docInfo.getVisibility();
330 |         if (visibility == Visibility.PRIVATE &&
331 |             !t.getInput().getName().equals(docInfo.getSourceName())) {
332 |           if (docInfo.isConstructor() &&
333 |               isValidPrivateConstructorAccess(parent)) {
334 |             return;
335 |           }
336 | 
337 |           compiler.report(
338 |               t.makeError(name, BAD_PRIVATE_GLOBAL_ACCESS,
339 |                   name.getString(), docInfo.getSourceName()));
340 |         }
341 |       }
342 |     }
343 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1323-1431
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1323 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1324 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1325 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1326 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1327 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1328 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1329 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1330 | 
1331 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1332 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1333 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1334 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1335 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1336 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1337 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1338 |         if (qVar != null) {
1339 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1340 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1341 |             return;
1342 |           }
1343 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1344 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1345 |           }
1346 |         }
1347 |       }
1348 | 
1349 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1350 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1351 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1352 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1353 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1354 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1355 |       //
1356 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1357 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1358 |       //
1359 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1360 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1361 | 
1362 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1363 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1364 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1365 |         // Determining type for #5
1366 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1367 |       }
1368 | 
1369 |       if (valueType == null) {
1370 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1371 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1372 |               n,
1373 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1374 |               ownerName));
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         return;
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       boolean inferred = true;
1381 |       if (info != null) {
1382 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3 + #4
1383 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1384 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1385 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1386 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1387 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1388 |       }
1389 | 
1390 |       if (inferred) {
1391 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1392 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1393 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1394 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1395 |       }
1396 | 
1397 |       if (!inferred) {
1398 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1399 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1400 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1401 |           // declared yet.
1402 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1403 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1404 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1405 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1406 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1407 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1408 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern, n);
1409 |           }
1410 |         }
1411 | 
1412 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1413 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1414 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1415 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1416 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1417 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1418 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1419 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1420 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1421 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1422 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1423 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1424 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1425 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1426 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1427 |                 true);
1428 |           }
1429 |         }
1430 |       }
1431 |     }

/**

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 644-670
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
644 |   @Override
645 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
646 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
647 |       return false;
648 |     }
649 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
650 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
654 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
655 |         return this == that;
656 |       }
657 |       return false;
658 |     }
659 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
660 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
661 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
662 |       }
663 |       return false;
664 |     }
665 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
669 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
670 |   }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 119-155
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
119 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
120 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
121 |                 Scope functionScope,
122 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
123 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
124 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
125 |     this.compiler = compiler;
126 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
127 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
128 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
129 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
130 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
131 | 
132 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
133 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
134 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
135 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
136 |       }
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
140 |     // type is VOID.
141 |     Iterator<Var> varIt =
142 |         functionScope.getDeclarativelyUnboundVarsWithoutTypes();
143 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
144 |       Var var = varIt.next();
145 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
146 |         continue;
147 |       }
148 | 
149 |       this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
150 |           var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
151 |     }
152 | 
153 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
154 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
155 |   }

228 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
229 |       boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccessControls.java
Original range: 196-208
Documentation:
/**
   * Normalize the type of a constructor, its instance, and its prototype
   * all down to the same type (the instance type).
   */
Method code:
196 |   private JSType normalizeClassType(JSType type) {
197 |     if (type == null || type.isUnknownType()) {
198 |       return type;
199 |     } else if (type.isConstructor()) {
200 |       return ((FunctionType) type).getInstanceType();
201 |     } else if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
202 |       FunctionType owner = ((FunctionPrototypeType) type).getOwnerFunction();
203 |       if (owner.isConstructor()) {
204 |         return owner.getInstanceType();
205 |       }
206 |     }
207 |     return type;
208 |   }

===== M0109 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1085-1223
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1085 |   Node parseInputs() {
1086 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1087 | 
1088 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1089 |     // individual file parse trees.
1090 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1091 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1092 |     }
1093 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1094 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1095 |     }
1096 | 
1097 |     // Parse main js sources.
1098 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1099 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1100 | 
1101 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1102 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1103 | 
1104 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1105 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1106 | 
1107 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1108 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1109 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1110 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1111 |     }
1112 | 
1113 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1114 | 
1115 |     try {
1116 |       // Parse externs sources.
1117 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1118 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1119 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1120 |           return null;
1121 |         }
1122 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1123 |       }
1124 | 
1125 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1126 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1127 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1128 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1129 | 
1130 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1131 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1132 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1133 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1134 |           }
1135 |         }
1136 | 
1137 |         try {
1138 |           inputs =
1139 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1140 |               .manageDependencies(
1141 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1142 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1143 |           report(JSError.make(
1144 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1145 |           return null;
1146 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1147 |           report(JSError.make(
1148 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1149 |           return null;
1150 |         }
1151 |       }
1152 | 
1153 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1154 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1155 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1156 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1157 | 
1158 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1159 |         if (n == null) {
1160 |           continue;
1161 |         }
1162 | 
1163 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1164 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1165 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1166 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1167 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1168 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1169 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1170 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1171 | 
1172 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1173 | 
1174 |             externs.add(input);
1175 |             staleInputs = true;
1176 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1177 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1178 |             staleInputs = true;
1179 |           }
1180 |         }
1181 |       }
1182 | 
1183 |       if (staleInputs) {
1184 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1185 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1186 |       }
1187 | 
1188 |       // Build the AST.
1189 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1190 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1191 |         if (n == null) {
1192 |           continue;
1193 |         }
1194 | 
1195 |         if (devMode) {
1196 |           runSanityCheck();
1197 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1198 |             return null;
1199 |           }
1200 |         }
1201 | 
1202 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1203 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1204 | 
1205 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1206 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1207 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1208 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1209 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1210 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1211 |         }
1212 | 
1213 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1214 |       }
1215 | 
1216 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1217 |         return null;
1218 |       }
1219 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1220 |     } finally {
1221 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1222 |     }
1223 |   }

===== M0115 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */

1758 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
1759 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1760 |     if (info == null) {
1761 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
1762 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
1763 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1764 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();

1766 |           info = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
1767 |         }
1768 |       } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1769 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();

1771 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1772 |       }
1773 |     }
1774 |     return info;
1775 |   }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 413-441
Documentation:

Method code:
413 |     @Override
414 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
415 |         Node parent) {
416 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
417 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
418 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
422 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
423 |       boolean descend = parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
424 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
425 | 
426 |       if (descend) {
427 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
428 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
429 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
430 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
431 |                child != null;
432 |                child = child.getNext()) {
433 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
434 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
435 |             }
436 |           }
437 |         }
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       return descend;
441 |     }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 786-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 900-921
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
901 |     switch (n.getType()) {
902 |       case Token.CALL:
903 |       case Token.GETPROP:
904 |       case Token.GETELEM:
905 |       case Token.THROW:
906 |       case Token.NEW:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
908 |       case Token.INC:
909 |       case Token.DEC:
910 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
911 |         return true;
912 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
913 |         return false;
914 |     }
915 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
916 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
917 |         return true;
918 |       }
919 |     }
920 |     return false;
921 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 166-206
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
166 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
167 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
168 |     switch (n.getType()) {
169 |       case Token.STRING:
170 |         return n.getString();
171 | 
172 |       case Token.NAME:
173 |         String name = n.getString();
174 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
175 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
176 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
177 |           return name;
178 |         }
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.NUMBER:
182 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
183 | 
184 |       case Token.FALSE:
185 |       case Token.TRUE:
186 |       case Token.NULL:
187 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
188 | 
189 |       case Token.VOID:
190 |         return "undefined";
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NOT:
193 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
194 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
195 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
196 |         }
197 |         break;
198 | 
199 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
200 |         return arrayToString(n);
201 | 
202 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
203 |         return "[object Object]";
204 |     }
205 |     return null;
206 |   }

/**
   * Looks up a type by name. To allow for forward references to types, an
   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.
   *
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name resolution.
   * @param jsTypeName The name string.
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file where this reference appears.
   * @param lineno The line number of the reference.
   * @return a NamedType if the string argument is not one of the known types,
   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.
   */
Method code:
841 |   public JSType getType(StaticScope<JSType> scope, String jsTypeName,
842 |       String sourceName, int lineno, int charno) {

846 |       // interning.

848 |           new NamedType(this, jsTypeName, sourceName, lineno, charno);
849 |       unresolvedNamedTypes.put(scope, namedType);
850 |       type = namedType;

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 381-399
Documentation:

Method code:
381 |   private void checkNoTypeCheckSection(Node n, boolean enterSection) {
382 |     switch (n.getType()) {
383 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
384 |       case Token.BLOCK:
385 |       case Token.VAR:
386 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
387 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
388 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
389 |         if (info != null && info.isNoTypeCheck()) {
390 |           if (enterSection) {
391 |             noTypeCheckSection++;
392 |           } else {
393 |             noTypeCheckSection--;
394 |           }
395 |         }
396 |         validator.setShouldReport(noTypeCheckSection == 0);
397 |         break;
398 |     }
399 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 311-341
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
311 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
312 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
313 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
314 |       return this;
315 |     }
316 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
317 |     if (returnType == null) {
318 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
319 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
320 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
321 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
322 |         int cType = current.getType();
323 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null) {
324 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
325 |           break;
326 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
327 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
328 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
329 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
330 |             worklist.add(child);
331 |           }
332 |         }
333 |       }
334 | 
335 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
336 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
337 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
338 |       }
339 |     }
340 |     return this;
341 |   }

365 |       for (com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Node child : n) {

367 |       }

369 |     }

347 |   public Scope processForTesting(Node externsRoot, Node jsRoot) {

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 774-873
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
774 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
775 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
776 |         @Nullable String name,
777 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
778 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
779 | 
780 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
781 | 
782 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
783 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
784 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
785 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
786 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = (FunctionType) var.getType();
787 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
788 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
789 |             functionType = aliasedType;
790 | 
791 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
792 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
793 |             }
794 |           }
795 |         }
796 |       }
797 | 
798 |       if (functionType == null) {
799 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
800 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
801 |             rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
802 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
803 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
804 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
805 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
806 | 
807 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
808 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
809 | 
810 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
811 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
812 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
813 |             functionType = (FunctionType) type;
814 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
815 |           }
816 |         }
817 | 
818 |         if (functionType == null) {
819 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
820 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
821 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
822 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
823 |               lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
824 |             Var var = scope.getVar(
825 |                 lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
826 |             if (var != null) {
827 |               ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
828 |               if (ownerType != null) {
829 |                 String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
830 |                 overriddenPropType =
831 |                     findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
832 |               }
833 |             }
834 |           }
835 | 
836 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
837 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
838 |                   scope)
839 |               .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
840 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
841 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
842 |               .inferReturnType(info)
843 |               .inferInheritance(info);
844 | 
845 |           // Infer the context type.
846 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
847 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
848 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
849 |             Node objNode = lvalueNode.getFirstChild();
850 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
851 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
852 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
853 |               searchedForThisType = true;
854 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
855 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
856 |               searchedForThisType = true;
857 |             }
858 |           }
859 | 
860 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
861 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
862 |           }
863 | 
864 |           functionType = builder
865 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
866 |               .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
867 |               .buildAndRegister();
868 |         }
869 |       }
870 | 
871 |       // all done
872 |       return functionType;
873 |     }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 826-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
826 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
827 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
828 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
829 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
830 | 
831 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
832 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
833 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
834 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
835 | 
836 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
837 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
838 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
839 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
840 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
841 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
842 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
843 |         }
844 |       }
845 | 
846 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
847 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
848 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
849 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
850 |             property, rvalue);
851 |         return;
852 |       }
853 | 
854 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
855 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
856 |         checkEnumInitializer(
857 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
858 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
859 |         return;
860 |       }
861 | 
862 |       // object.prototype = ...;
863 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
864 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
865 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
866 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
867 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
868 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
869 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
870 |           }
871 |         } else {
872 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
873 |         }
874 |         return;
875 |       }
876 | 
877 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
878 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
879 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
880 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
881 | 
882 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
883 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
884 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
885 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
886 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
887 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
888 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
889 |             }
890 |           } else {
891 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
892 |           }
893 |           return;
894 |         }
895 |       }
896 | 
897 |       // object.property = ...;
898 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
899 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
900 |       if (type != null) {
901 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
902 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
903 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
904 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
905 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
906 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
907 |         }
908 |         return;
909 |       }
910 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
911 |       // variable with inferred type case
912 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
913 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
914 |       if (var != null) {
915 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
916 |           return;
917 |         }
918 |       }
919 |     }
920 | 
921 |     // fall through case
922 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
923 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
924 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
925 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
926 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
927 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
928 |     } else {
929 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
930 |     }
931 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1020 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1021 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1022 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1023 | 
1024 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.getType() == Token.NAME && scope.isGlobal();
1025 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1026 |           isGlobalVar &&
1027 |           (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
1028 |            parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1029 | 
1030 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1031 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1032 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1033 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1034 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1035 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1036 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1037 | 
1038 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1039 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1040 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1041 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       // declared in closest scope?
1046 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1047 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1048 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1049 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1050 |       } else {
1051 |         if (!inferred) {
1052 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1053 |         }
1054 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1055 |         boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
1056 |         Var newVar =
1057 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1058 | 
1059 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1060 |           ObjectType globalThis =
1061 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1062 |           if (inferred) {
1063 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1064 |                 type == null ?
1065 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1066 |                     type,
1067 |                 isExtern, n);
1068 |           } else {
1069 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern, n);
1070 |           }
1071 |         }
1072 | 
1073 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1074 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1075 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1076 |               (initialValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT ||
1077 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1078 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1079 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1080 |           }
1081 |         }
1082 | 
1083 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1084 |         if (type instanceof FunctionType &&
1085 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1086 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1087 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
1088 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1089 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1090 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1091 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1092 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1093 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1094 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1095 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1096 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1097 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1098 | 
1099 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1100 |             // it constructs itself.
1101 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1102 |                 !isExtern &&
1103 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1104 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1105 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1106 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1107 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1108 |                 // this.
1109 |                 variableName.equals(
1110 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1111 |               compiler.report(
1112 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1113 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1114 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1115 |                       variableName));
1116 |             }
1117 |           }
1118 |         }
1119 |       }
1120 | 
1121 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1122 |           && type instanceof FunctionType
1123 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1124 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1125 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1126 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1127 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1128 |         globalThisCtor
1129 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn(((FunctionType) type).getInstanceType());
1130 |       }
1131 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is

1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1166 |       }
1167 |     }

655 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
656 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
657 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 188-300
Documentation:

Method code:
188 |   @Override
189 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
190 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
191 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
192 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
193 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
194 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
195 |     // worthwhile.
196 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
197 |     Node condition = null;
198 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
199 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
200 | 
201 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
202 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
203 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
204 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
205 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
206 | 
207 |       switch (branch) {
208 |         case ON_TRUE:
209 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
210 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
211 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
212 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
213 | 
214 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
215 | 
216 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
217 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
218 |             }
219 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
220 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
221 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
222 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
223 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
224 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
225 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
226 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
227 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
228 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
229 |                 }
230 |               }
231 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
232 |             }
233 |             newScope = informed;
234 |             break;
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           // FALL THROUGH
238 | 
239 |         case ON_FALSE:
240 |           if (condition == null) {
241 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
242 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
243 |               condition = source;
244 | 
245 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
246 |               // of the loop.
247 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
248 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
249 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
250 |               }
251 |             }
252 |           }
253 | 
254 |           if (condition != null) {
255 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
256 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
257 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
258 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
259 |               // scope on false.
260 |               //
261 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
262 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
263 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
264 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
265 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
266 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
267 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
268 | 
269 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
272 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
273 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
274 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
275 |               }
276 |               newScope =
277 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
278 |                       condition,
279 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
280 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
281 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
282 |             } else {
283 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
284 |               // of the loop.
285 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
286 |                 conditionFlowScope =
287 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
288 |               }
289 |               newScope =
290 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
291 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
292 |             }
293 |           }
294 |           break;
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
298 |     }
299 |     return result;
300 |   }
