/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
581 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
582 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
583 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
584 |       return that.getLeastSupertype(this);
585 |     }
586 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
587 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1156-1204
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1156 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1157 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1158 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1159 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1160 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1161 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1162 |               (FunctionType) rValue.getJSType(), info, lValue)) {
1163 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1164 |       } else if (info != null) {
1165 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1166 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1167 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1168 |           } else {
1169 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1170 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1171 |           }
1172 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1173 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1174 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1175 |         } else {
1176 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1177 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1178 |             JSType knownType = null;
1179 |             if (rValue != null) {
1180 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1181 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1182 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1183 |               } else if (rValue.getType() == Token.OR) {
1184 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1185 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1186 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1187 |                 // reasons.
1188 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1189 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1190 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.getType() == Token.NAME
1191 |                     && lValue.getType() == Token.NAME
1192 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1193 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1194 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1195 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1196 |                 }
1197 |               }
1198 |             }
1199 |           }
1200 |         }
1201 |       }
1202 | 
1203 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1204 |     }

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-88

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         }
70 | 
71 |         if (info != null) {
72 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
73 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
74 | 
75 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
76 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
77 |             if (guard != null) {
78 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
79 |               if (newLevel != null) {
80 |                 return newLevel;
81 |               }
82 |             }
83 |           }
84 |         }
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |     return null;
88 |   }

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckAccessControls.getClassOfMethod(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccessControls.java
Original range: 162-190
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the type of the class that "owns" a method, or null if
   * we know that its un-owned.
   */
Method code:
162 |   private JSType getClassOfMethod(Node n, Node parent) {
163 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
164 |       Node lValue = parent.getFirstChild();
165 |       if (lValue.isQualifiedName()) {
166 |         if (lValue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
167 |           // We have an assignment of the form "a.b = ...".
168 |           JSType lValueType = lValue.getJSType();
169 |           if (lValueType != null && lValueType.isConstructor()) {
170 |             // If a.b is a constructor, then everything in this function
171 |             // belongs to the "a.b" type.
172 |             return ((FunctionType) lValueType).getInstanceType();
173 |           } else {
174 |             // If a.b is not a constructor, then treat this as a method
175 |             // of whatever type is on "a".
176 |             return normalizeClassType(lValue.getFirstChild().getJSType());
177 |           }
178 |         } else {
179 |           // We have an assignment of the form "a = ...", so pull the
180 |           // type off the "a".
181 |           return normalizeClassType(lValue.getJSType());
182 |         }
183 |       }
184 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n) ||
185 |                parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
186 |       return normalizeClassType(n.getJSType());
187 |     }
188 | 
189 |     return null;
190 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 684-720
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
684 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
685 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
686 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
687 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
688 | 
689 |     if (existingType != null) {
690 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
691 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
692 |         FunctionType existingFn =
693 |             isInstanceObject ?
694 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
695 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
696 | 
697 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
698 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
699 |         }
700 | 
701 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
702 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
703 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
704 |         }
705 | 
706 |         return existingFn;
707 |       } else {
708 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
709 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
710 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
711 |       }
712 |     }
713 | 
714 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
715 | 
716 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
717 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
718 |     }
719 |     return fnType;
720 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1177-1206
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1177 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1178 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1179 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1180 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1181 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1182 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1183 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1184 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1185 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1186 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1187 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1188 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1189 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1190 |       return false;
1191 |     }
1192 | 
1193 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1194 |     if (type == null) {
1195 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1196 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1197 |       if (var != null) {
1198 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1199 |         if (varType != null) {
1200 |           type = varType;
1201 |         }
1202 |       }
1203 |     }
1204 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1205 |     return true;
1206 |   }

/**

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
445 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
446 | 
447 |       switch (n.getType()) {
448 |         case Token.CALL:
449 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
450 |           break;
451 | 
452 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
453 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
454 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
455 |           }
456 | 
457 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
458 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
459 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
460 |           }
461 |           break;
462 | 
463 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
464 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
465 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
466 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
467 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
468 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
469 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
470 |           }
471 |           break;
472 | 
473 |         case Token.CATCH:
474 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
475 |           break;
476 | 
477 |         case Token.VAR:
478 |           defineVar(n, parent);
479 |           break;
480 | 
481 |         case Token.GETPROP:
482 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
483 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
484 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
485 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
486 |           }
487 |           break;
488 |       }
489 |     }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 237-288
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
237 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
238 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
239 |     if (oldType == null) {
240 |       return this;
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
244 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
245 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
246 |       // Not a function literal.
247 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
248 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
249 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
250 |       }
251 |     } else {
252 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
253 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
254 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
255 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
256 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
257 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
258 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
259 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
260 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
261 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
262 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
263 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
264 | 
265 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
266 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
267 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
268 | 
269 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
270 |           // arguments.
271 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
272 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
273 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
274 |           }
275 |         } else {
276 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
277 |               paramBuilder,
278 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
279 |               warnedAboutArgList,
280 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
281 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
282 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
283 |         }
284 |       }
285 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
286 |     }
287 |     return this;
288 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 469-538
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
469 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
470 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
471 |     if (argsParent == null) {
472 |       if (info == null) {
473 |         return this;
474 |       } else {
475 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
476 |       }
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     // arguments
480 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
481 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
482 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
486 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
487 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
488 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
489 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
490 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
491 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
492 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
493 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
494 | 
495 |       // type from JSDocInfo
496 |       JSType parameterType = null;
497 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
498 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
499 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
500 |         parameterType =
501 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
502 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
503 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
504 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
505 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
506 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
507 |       } else {
508 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
509 |       }
510 | 
511 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
512 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
513 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
514 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
515 |         }
516 |         foundTemplateType = true;
517 |       }
518 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
519 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
520 |           isOptionalParam,
521 |           isVarArgs);
522 | 
523 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
524 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
525 |       }
526 |     }
527 | 
528 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
529 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
530 |     }
531 | 
532 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
533 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
537 |     return this;
538 |   }

67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 429-473
Documentation:

Method code:
429 |   @Override
430 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
431 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
432 |       return true;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // Union types
436 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
437 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
438 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
439 |       return false;
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     // record types
443 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
444 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
445 |     }
446 | 
447 |     // Interfaces
448 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
449 |     // to the interface instance.
450 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
451 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
452 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
453 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
454 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
455 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
456 |           return true;
457 |         }
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // other prototype based objects
462 |     if (that != null) {
463 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
464 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
465 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
466 |         // to avoid guessing.
467 |         return true;
468 |       }
469 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
470 |     }
471 | 
472 |     return false;
473 |   }

444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);

   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0427 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1011-1085
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1011 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1012 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1013 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1014 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1015 |     // already.
1016 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1017 |       return;
1018 |     }
1019 | 
1020 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1021 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1022 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1023 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1024 | 
1025 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1026 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1027 |       for (JSType implementedInterface : ctorType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1028 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1029 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1030 |           continue;
1031 |         }
1032 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1033 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1034 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1035 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1036 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1037 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1038 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1039 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1040 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1041 |           // property
1042 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1043 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1044 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1045 |         }
1046 |       }
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty) {
1050 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1051 |       return;
1052 |     }
1053 | 
1054 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1055 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1056 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1057 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1058 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1059 |       // property
1060 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1061 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1062 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1063 |     }
1064 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1065 |       // there's no @override to check
1066 |       return;
1067 |     }
1068 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1069 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1070 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1071 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1072 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1073 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1074 |         compiler.report(
1075 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1076 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1077 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1078 |       }
1079 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1080 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1081 |       compiler.report(
1082 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1083 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1084 |     }
1085 |   }

===== M0428 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 418-450
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
418 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
419 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
420 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
421 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
422 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
423 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
424 |     }
425 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
426 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
427 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
428 |     } else if (owner != null &&
429 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
430 |       // If the function is of the form:
431 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
432 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
433 |       // if it's of the form:
434 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
435 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
436 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
437 |       Var ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerTypeName);
438 |       JSType ownerType = ownerVar == null ? null : ownerVar.getType();
439 |       FunctionType ownerFnType = ownerType instanceof FunctionType ?
440 |           (FunctionType) ownerType : null;
441 |       ObjectType instType =
442 |           ownerFnType == null || ownerFnType.isOrdinaryFunction() ?
443 |           null : ownerFnType.getInstanceType();
444 |       if (instType != null) {
445 |         thisType = instType;
446 |       }
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return this;
450 |   }

===== M0443 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1775-1799
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1775 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1776 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1777 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1778 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1779 |             type.isUnknownType());
1780 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1781 |     if (info != null) {
1782 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1783 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1784 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1785 |         type = infoType;
1786 |       }
1787 | 
1788 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1789 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1790 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1791 |         compiler.report(
1792 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1793 |       }
1794 |     }
1795 | 
1796 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1797 |       n.setJSType(type);
1798 |     }
1799 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

===== M0458 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1395-1439
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1395 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1396 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1397 | 
1398 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1399 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1400 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1401 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1402 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1403 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1404 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1405 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1406 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1407 |         compiler.report(
1408 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1409 |       }
1410 | 
1411 |       if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1412 |         if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1413 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1414 |               CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1415 |         }
1416 |       } else {
1417 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1418 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1419 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1420 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1421 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1422 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1423 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1424 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1425 |               badImplementedType = true;
1426 |             }
1427 |           } else {
1428 |             badImplementedType = true;
1429 |           }
1430 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1431 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1432 |           }
1433 |         }
1434 |       }
1435 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1436 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1437 |       }
1438 |     }
1439 |   }

===== M0464 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 892-937
Documentation:

Method code:
892 |   @Override
893 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
894 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this);
895 | 
896 |     call = (ArrowType) safeResolve(call, t, scope);
897 |     prototype = (FunctionPrototypeType) safeResolve(prototype, t, scope);
898 | 
899 |     // Warning about typeOfThis if it doesn't resolve to an ObjectType
900 |     // is handled further upstream.
901 |     //
902 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
903 |     //
904 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
905 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
906 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
907 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
908 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
909 |     // the name for them.
910 |     JSType maybeTypeOfThis = safeResolve(typeOfThis, t, scope);
911 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
912 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
913 |     }
914 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
915 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
916 |     }
917 | 
918 |     boolean changed = false;
919 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
920 |         ImmutableList.builder();
921 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
922 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
923 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
924 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
925 |     }
926 |     if (changed) {
927 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
928 |     }
929 | 
930 |     if (subTypes != null) {
931 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
932 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));
933 |       }
934 |     }
935 | 
936 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
937 |   }

631 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
632 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     FunctionType fnType;
636 |     if (isConstructor) {
637 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
638 |     } else if (isInterface) {
639 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(fnName, sourceNode);
640 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
641 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
642 |       }
643 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
644 |     } else {
645 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
646 |           .withName(fnName)
647 |           .withSourceNode(sourceNode)
648 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
649 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
650 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
651 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
652 |           .build();
653 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
657 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
658 |     }
659 | 
660 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
661 | 
662 |     return fnType;
663 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 347-391
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
347 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
348 |     if (info != null) {
349 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
350 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
351 | 
352 |       // base type
353 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
354 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
355 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
356 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
358 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
360 |           }
361 |         } else {
362 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
363 |         }
364 |       }
365 | 
366 |       // implemented interfaces
367 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
368 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
369 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
370 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
371 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
372 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
373 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
374 |           }
375 |         }
376 |         if (baseType != null) {
377 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
378 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
379 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
380 |             Iterables.addAll(
381 |                 implementedInterfaces,
382 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
383 |           }
384 |         }
385 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
386 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
387 |       }
388 |     }
389 | 
390 |     return this;
391 |   }

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckAccessControls.checkConstantProperty(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccessControls.java
Original range: 350-400
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given property with @const tag got reassigned
   * @param t The current traversal.
   * @param getprop The getprop node.
   */
Method code:
350 |   private void checkConstantProperty(NodeTraversal t,
351 |       Node getprop) {
352 |     // Check whether the property is modified
353 |     Node parent = getprop.getParent();
354 |     if (!(NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == getprop)
355 |         && (parent.getType() != Token.INC) && (parent.getType() != Token.DEC)) {
356 |       return;
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     ObjectType objectType =
360 |       ObjectType.cast(dereference(getprop.getFirstChild().getJSType()));
361 |     String propertyName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
362 | 
363 |     // Check whether constant properties are reassigned
364 |     if (objectType != null) {
365 |       ObjectType oType = objectType;
366 |       while (oType != null) {
367 |         if (oType.hasReferenceName()) {
368 |           if (initializedConstantProperties.containsEntry(
369 |                   oType.getReferenceName(), propertyName)) {
370 |             compiler.report(
371 |                 t.makeError(getprop, CONST_PROPERTY_REASSIGNED_VALUE,
372 |                     propertyName));
373 |             break;
374 |           }
375 |         }
376 |         oType = oType.getImplicitPrototype();
377 |       }
378 | 
379 |       JSDocInfo info = objectType.getOwnPropertyJSDocInfo(propertyName);
380 |       if (info != null && info.isConstant()
381 |           && objectType.hasReferenceName()) {
382 |         initializedConstantProperties.put(objectType.getReferenceName(),
383 |             propertyName);
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       // Add the prototype when we're looking at an instance object
387 |       if (objectType.isInstanceType()) {
388 |         ObjectType prototype = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
389 |         if (prototype != null) {
390 |           JSDocInfo prototypeInfo
391 |             = prototype.getOwnPropertyJSDocInfo(propertyName);
392 |           if (prototypeInfo != null && prototypeInfo.isConstant()
393 |               && prototype.hasReferenceName()) {
394 |             initializedConstantProperties.put(prototype.getReferenceName(),
395 |                 propertyName);
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |       }
399 |     }
400 |   }

===== M0546 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 242-268
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
242 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
243 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
244 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
245 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
246 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
247 | 
248 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
249 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
250 |       // if (this.x == null) {
251 |       //   this.initializeX();
252 |       //   this.x.foo();
253 |       // }
254 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
255 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
256 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
257 |       //
258 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
259 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
260 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
265 |       return false;
266 |     }
267 |     return true;
268 |   }

===== M0547 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckAccessControls.checkPropertyVisibility(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccessControls.java
Original range: 407-500
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given property is visible in the current context.
   * @param t The current traversal.
   * @param getprop The getprop node.
   */
Method code:
407 |   private void checkPropertyVisibility(NodeTraversal t,
408 |       Node getprop, Node parent) {
409 |     ObjectType objectType =
410 |         ObjectType.cast(dereference(getprop.getFirstChild().getJSType()));
411 |     String propertyName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
412 | 
413 |     if (objectType != null) {
414 |       // Is this a normal property access, or are we trying to override
415 |       // an existing property?
416 |       boolean isOverride = t.inGlobalScope() &&
417 |           parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
418 |           parent.getFirstChild() == getprop;
419 | 
420 |       // Find the lowest property defined on a class with visibility
421 |       // information.
422 |       if (isOverride) {
423 |         objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
424 |       }
425 |       JSDocInfo docInfo = null;
426 |       for (; objectType != null;
427 |            objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype()) {
428 |         docInfo = objectType.getOwnPropertyJSDocInfo(propertyName);
429 |         if (docInfo != null &&
430 |             docInfo.getVisibility() != Visibility.INHERITED) {
431 |           break;
432 |         }
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       if (objectType == null) {
436 |         // We couldn't find a visibility modifier; assume it's public.
437 |         return;
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       boolean sameInput =
441 |           t.getInput().getName().equals(docInfo.getSourceName());
442 |       Visibility visibility = docInfo.getVisibility();
443 |       JSType ownerType = normalizeClassType(objectType);
444 |       if (isOverride) {
445 |         // Check an ASSIGN statement that's trying to override a property
446 |         // on a superclass.
447 |         JSDocInfo overridingInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
448 |         Visibility overridingVisibility = overridingInfo == null ?
449 |             Visibility.INHERITED : overridingInfo.getVisibility();
450 | 
451 |         // Check that (a) the property *can* be overridden, and
452 |         // (b) that the visibility of the override is the same as the
453 |         // visibility of the original property.
454 |         if (visibility == Visibility.PRIVATE && !sameInput) {
455 |           compiler.report(
456 |               t.makeError(getprop, PRIVATE_OVERRIDE,
457 |                   objectType.toString()));
458 |         } else if (overridingVisibility != Visibility.INHERITED &&
459 |             overridingVisibility != visibility) {
460 |           compiler.report(
461 |               t.makeError(getprop, VISIBILITY_MISMATCH,
462 |                   visibility.name(), objectType.toString(),
463 |                   overridingVisibility.name()));
464 |         }
465 |       } else {
466 |         if (sameInput) {
467 |           // private access is always allowed in the same file.
468 |           return;
469 |         } else if (visibility == Visibility.PRIVATE &&
470 |             (currentClass == null || ownerType.differsFrom(currentClass))) {
471 |           if (docInfo.isConstructor() &&
472 |               isValidPrivateConstructorAccess(parent)) {
473 |             return;
474 |           }
475 | 
476 |           // private access is not allowed outside the file from a different
477 |           // enclosing class.
478 |           compiler.report(
479 |               t.makeError(getprop,
480 |                   BAD_PRIVATE_PROPERTY_ACCESS,
481 |                   propertyName,
482 |                   validator.getReadableJSTypeName(
483 |                       getprop.getFirstChild(), true)));
484 |         } else if (visibility == Visibility.PROTECTED) {
485 |           // There are 3 types of legal accesses of a protected property:
486 |           // 1) Accesses in the same file
487 |           // 2) Overriding the property in a subclass
488 |           // 3) Accessing the property from inside a subclass
489 |           // The first two have already been checked for.
490 |           if (currentClass == null || !currentClass.isSubtype(ownerType)) {
491 |             compiler.report(
492 |                 t.makeError(getprop,  BAD_PROTECTED_PROPERTY_ACCESS,
493 |                     propertyName,
494 |                     validator.getReadableJSTypeName(
495 |                         getprop.getFirstChild(), true)));
496 |           }
497 |         }
498 |       }
499 |     }
500 |   }

414 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
415 |       List<JSModule> modules) {

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.

954 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);

956 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
957 |       }
958 |       return resolveResult;
959 |     }
960 |     resolved = true;
961 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
962 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
963 |     return resolveResult;
964 |   }

===== M0581 =====

Documentation:

Method code:
35 |     @Override
36 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
37 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
38 |       int size = values.size();
39 |       if (size == 1) {
40 |         return values.get(0);
41 |       } else if (size == 2) {
42 |         return apply(values.get(0), values.get(1));
43 |       } else {
44 |         int mid = computeMidPoint(size);
45 |         return apply(
46 |             apply(values.subList(0, mid)),
47 |             apply(values.subList(mid, size)));
48 |       }
49 |     }

===== M0596 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 302-463
Documentation:

Method code:
302 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
303 |     switch (n.getType()) {
304 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
305 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
306 |         break;
307 | 
308 |       case Token.NAME:
309 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
310 |         break;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.GETPROP:
313 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
314 |         break;
315 | 
316 |       case Token.AND:
317 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
318 |             .createChildFlowScope();
319 |         break;
320 | 
321 |       case Token.OR:
322 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
323 |             .createChildFlowScope();
324 |         break;
325 | 
326 |       case Token.HOOK:
327 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
328 |         break;
329 | 
330 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
331 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.CALL:
335 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
336 |         break;
337 | 
338 |       case Token.NEW:
339 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
343 |       case Token.ADD:
344 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.POS:
348 |       case Token.NEG:
349 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
350 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
351 |         break;
352 | 
353 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
354 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
355 |         break;
356 | 
357 |       case Token.THIS:
358 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
359 |         break;
360 | 
361 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
362 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
363 |       case Token.LSH:
364 |       case Token.RSH:
365 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
366 |       case Token.URSH:
367 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
374 |       case Token.DIV:
375 |       case Token.MOD:
376 |       case Token.BITAND:
377 |       case Token.BITXOR:
378 |       case Token.BITOR:
379 |       case Token.MUL:
380 |       case Token.SUB:
381 |       case Token.DEC:
382 |       case Token.INC:
383 |       case Token.BITNOT:
384 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
385 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
386 |         break;
387 | 
388 |       case Token.LP:
389 |       case Token.GET_REF:
390 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
391 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
392 |         break;
393 | 
394 |       case Token.COMMA:
395 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
396 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
397 |         break;
398 | 
399 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
400 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
401 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
402 |         break;
403 | 
404 |       case Token.DELPROP:
405 |       case Token.LT:
406 |       case Token.LE:
407 |       case Token.GT:
408 |       case Token.GE:
409 |       case Token.NOT:
410 |       case Token.EQ:
411 |       case Token.NE:
412 |       case Token.SHEQ:
413 |       case Token.SHNE:
414 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
415 |       case Token.IN:
416 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
417 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.GETELEM:
421 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
425 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
426 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
427 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.SWITCH:
432 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.VAR:
436 |       case Token.RETURN:
437 |       case Token.THROW:
438 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.CATCH:
442 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
443 |         break;
444 |     }
445 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
446 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
447 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
448 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
449 | 
450 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
451 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
452 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
453 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
454 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
455 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
456 |         }
457 | 
458 |         n.setJSType(castType);
459 |       }
460 |     }
461 | 
462 |     return scope;
463 |   }

===== M0610 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.register(JSType,String)

Method code:
581 |   private void register(JSType type, String name) {
582 |     namesToTypes.put(name, type);
583 |

===== M0618 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 545-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
545 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
546 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
547 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
548 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
549 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
550 |     if (objectType == null) {
551 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
552 |     } else {
553 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
554 |         return;
555 |       }
556 | 
557 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
558 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
559 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
560 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
561 |         //
562 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
563 |         //    inferred type.
564 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
565 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
566 |         //    define it.
567 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
568 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
569 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
570 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
571 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
572 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
573 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
574 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
575 |           } else {
576 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
577 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
578 |           }
579 |         } else {
580 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
581 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
582 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
583 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
584 |           } else {
585 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
586 |           }
587 |         }
588 |       }
589 |     }
590 |   }

===== M0634 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }
