===== M0636 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 169-913
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
169 |   boolean parse() {
170 |     int lineno;
171 |     int charno;
172 | 
173 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
174 |     JSTypeExpression type;
175 | 
176 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
177 |     skipEOLs();
178 | 
179 |     JsDocToken token = next();
180 | 
181 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
182 | 
183 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
184 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
185 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
186 |       token = blockInfo.token;
187 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
188 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
189 |       }
190 |     } else {
191 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
192 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
193 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
194 |         // what it was.
195 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
200 |     retry: for (;;) {
201 |       switch (token) {
202 |         case ANNOTATION:
203 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
204 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
205 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
206 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
207 | 
208 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
209 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
210 |             if (annotation == null) {
211 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
212 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
213 |             } else {
214 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
215 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
216 | 
217 |               switch (annotation) {
218 |                 case AUTHOR:
219 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
220 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
221 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
222 | 
223 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
224 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
225 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                     } else {
227 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
228 |                     }
229 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
230 |                   } else {
231 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
232 |                   }
233 |                   continue retry;
234 | 
235 |                 case CONSTANT:
236 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
237 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
238 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                   }
240 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
241 |                   continue retry;
242 | 
243 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
244 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
245 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
246 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
247 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
248 |                     } else {
249 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
250 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                     }
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case DEPRECATED:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 | 
262 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
263 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
264 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
265 | 
266 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
267 | 
268 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
269 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
270 |                   }
271 | 
272 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
273 |                   continue retry;
274 | 
275 |                 case INTERFACE:
276 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
277 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
278 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
279 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                     } else {
281 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
282 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
283 |                     }
284 |                   }
285 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
286 |                   continue retry;
287 | 
288 |                 case DESC:
289 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
290 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
291 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
292 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
293 |                     continue retry;
294 |                   } else {
295 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
296 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
297 | 
298 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
299 | 
300 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
301 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
302 |                     continue retry;
303 |                   }
304 | 
305 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
306 |                   String fileOverview = "";
307 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
308 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
309 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
310 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
311 | 
312 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
313 | 
314 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
315 |                   } else {
316 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
317 |                   }
318 | 
319 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
320 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
321 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
322 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
323 |                   }
324 |                   continue retry;
325 | 
326 |                 case LICENSE:
327 |                 case PRESERVE:

851 |                         }
852 |                         break;

858 |                         }

864 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
865 |                         }

877 |         case EOC:
878 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
879 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
880 |           }

884 |         case EOF:

888 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

899 |         default:

901 |             token = next();
902 |             continue retry;
903 |           } else {
904 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
905 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
906 |             continue retry;
907 |           }
908 |       }
909 | 
910 |       // next token
911 |       token = next();
912 |     }
913 |   }

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

/**
   * Creates a map to make looking up an input by name fast. Also checks for
   * duplicate inputs.
   */
Method code:
442 |   void initInputsByNameMap() {
443 |     inputsByName = new HashMap<String, CompilerInput>();
444 |     for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
445 |       String name = input.getName();
446 |       if (!inputsByName.containsKey(name)) {
447 |         inputsByName.put(name, input);
448 |       } else {
449 |         report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_EXTERN_INPUT, name));
450 |       }
451 |     }
452 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
453 |       String name = input.getName();
454 |       if (!inputsByName.containsKey(name)) {
455 |         inputsByName.put(name, input);
456 |       } else {
457 |         report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_INPUT, name));
458 |       }
459 |     }
460 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0678 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0705 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1091-1113
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1091 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1092 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1093 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1094 | 
1095 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1096 |     // and break the cycle.
1097 |     while (true) {
1098 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1099 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1100 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1101 |         return false;
1102 |       }
1103 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1104 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1105 |         return true;
1106 |       }
1107 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1108 |       if (ctor == null) {
1109 |         return false;
1110 |       }
1111 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1112 |     }
1113 |   }

169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {

207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }

217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:

238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:

242 |       case Token.NAME:

244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1159-1167
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1159 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1160 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1161 |       return -1;
1162 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1163 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1164 |     } else {
1165 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1166 |     }
1167 |   }

/**
   * Checks the given GETPROP node to ensure that access restrictions are
   * obeyed.
   */
Method code:
287 |   private void checkPropertyDeprecation(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
288 |     // Don't bother checking constructors.
289 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.NEW) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 |

302 |           shouldEmitDeprecationWarning(t, n, parent)) {

109 |   @Override
110 |   public void inferQualifiedSlot(String symbol, JSType bottomType,
111 |       JSType inferredType) {
112 |     Scope functionScope = getFunctionScope();
113 |     if (functionScope.isLocal()) {
114 |       if (functionScope.getVar(symbol) == null && !functionScope.isBottom()) {
115 |         // When we enter a local scope, many qualified names are

===== M0743 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 494-540
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
494 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
495 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
496 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
497 |     switch (left.getType()) {
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String varName = left.getString();
500 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
501 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
502 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
503 |         }
504 | 
505 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
506 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
507 |         // This has two purposes:
508 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
509 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
510 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
511 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
512 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
513 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
514 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
515 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
516 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
517 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
518 |         }
519 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
520 |             resultType : null);
521 | 
522 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
523 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
524 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
525 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
526 |         }
527 |         break;
528 |       case Token.GETPROP:
529 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
530 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
531 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
532 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
533 |               resultType);
534 |         }
535 | 
536 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
537 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
538 |         break;
539 |     }
540 |   }

/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
687 |   @Override
688 |   public String toString() {
689 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
690 |       return "Function";
691 |     }
692 | 
693 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
694 |     b.append("function (");
695 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
696 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
697 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
698 |       if (isConstructor()) {
699 |         b.append("new:");
700 |       } else {
701 |         b.append("this:");
702 |       }
703 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
704 |     }
705 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
706 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
707 |         b.append(", ");
708 |       }
709 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
710 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
711 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
712 |       } else {
713 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
714 |       }
715 |       p = p.getNext();
716 |       while (p != null) {
717 |         b.append(", ");
718 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
719 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
720 |         } else {
721 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
722 |         }
723 |         p = p.getNext();
724 |       }
725 |     }
726 |     b.append("): ");
727 |     b.append(call.returnType);
728 |     return b.toString();
729 |   }

Method code:
408 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
409 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
410 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
411 |     switch (n.getType()) {
412 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
413 |         // normal type checking
414 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
415 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
416 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
417 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
418 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
419 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
420 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
421 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
422 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
423 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
424 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
425 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
426 |         }
427 | 
428 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
429 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
430 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
431 |         break;
432 |     }
433 |     return true;
434 |   }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1888 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1889 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1890 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {

1892 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1893 |       if (result.last != null) {

1895 |       }

1897 |         result.first = n2clone;
1898 |       }
1899 |       result.last = n2clone;
1900 |     }
1901 |     return result;
1902 |   }

   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place

/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1217 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1218 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1219 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1220 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1221 |       return;
1222 |     }
1223 | 
1224 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1225 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1226 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1227 |     // and what isn't.
1228 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1229 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1230 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1231 | 
1232 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1233 |     // non-object.
1234 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1235 |             childType + " has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1236 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1237 |       return;
1238 |     }
1239 | 
1240 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1241 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1242 |   }

350 |   public String toString() {
351 |     if (hasReferenceName()) {
352 |       return getReferenceName();
353 |     } else if (prettyPrint) {
354 |       // Use a tree set so that the properties are sorted.
355 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
356 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
357 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
358 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
359 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
360 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
361 |       }
362 | 
363 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
364 |       sb.append("{");
365 | 
366 |       int i = 0;
367 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
368 |         if (i > 0) {
369 |           sb.append(", ");
370 |         }
371 | 
372 |         sb.append(property);
373 |         sb.append(": ");
374 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toString());
375 | 
376 |         ++i;
377 |         if (i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
378 |           sb.append(", ...");
379 |           break;
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       sb.append("}");
384 |       return sb.toString();
385 |     } else {
386 |       return "{...}";
387 |     }
388 |   }

===== M0797 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 746-788
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
746 |   @Override
747 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
748 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
749 |       return true;
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
753 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
754 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
755 |         return true;
756 |       }
757 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
758 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
759 |         return false;
760 |       }
761 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
762 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
763 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
764 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
765 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
766 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
767 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
768 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
769 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
770 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
771 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
772 | 
773 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
774 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
775 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
776 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
777 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
778 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
779 | 
780 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
781 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
782 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
783 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
784 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
785 |     }
786 | 
787 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
788 |   }

   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
447 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
448 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
449 |          current != null;
450 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
451 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
452 |         return true;
453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return false;
456 |   }

   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.

Method code:
2822 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2823 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2824 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2825 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2826 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2827 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2828 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {

2832 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2833 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2834 |       }
2835 |     }
2836 |     return fnInfo;

===== M0813 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1452-1482
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1453 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1454 |         Node n = stub.node;
1455 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1456 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1459 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1460 | 
1461 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1462 |           continue;
1463 |         }
1464 | 
1465 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1466 |         // in the type registry.
1467 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1468 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1469 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1470 | 
1471 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1472 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1473 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1474 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1475 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1476 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern, n);
1477 |         } else {
1478 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1479 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1480 |         }
1481 |       }
1482 |     }

Method code:
491 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
492 |       switch (n.getType()) {
493 |         case Token.NULL:
494 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
495 |           break;
496 | 
497 |         case Token.VOID:
498 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
499 |           break;
500 | 
501 |         case Token.STRING:
502 |           // Defer keys to the Token.OBJECTLIT case
503 |           if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
504 |             n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
505 |           }
506 |           break;
507 | 
508 |         case Token.NUMBER:
509 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
510 |           break;
511 | 
512 |         case Token.TRUE:
513 |         case Token.FALSE:
514 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
515 |           break;
516 | 
517 |         case Token.REGEXP:
518 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
519 |           break;
520 | 
521 |         case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
522 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
523 |           break;
524 | 
525 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
526 |           defineObjectLiteral(t, n);
527 |           break;
528 | 
529 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
530 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
531 |       }
532 |     }

===== M0825 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 180-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
180 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
181 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
182 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
183 |     // show up in the type registry.
184 |     Scope newScope = null;
185 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
186 |     if (parent == null) {
187 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
188 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
189 | 
190 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
191 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
192 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
193 |     } else {
194 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
195 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
196 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
197 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
198 |     }
199 | 
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
201 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
202 | 
203 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
204 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
205 |     // build properties on.
206 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
207 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
208 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
209 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
210 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
211 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
212 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
213 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
214 |         }
215 |       }
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     if (parent == null) {
219 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
220 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
221 |     }
222 |     return newScope;
223 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
446 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
447 |     JSType childType;
448 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
449 |     Node left, right;
450 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
451 |     boolean typeable = true;
452 | 
453 |     switch (n.getType()) {
454 |       case Token.NAME:
455 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.LP:
459 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
460 |         // ignored here.

463 |         } else {

466 |         break;

475 |         break;

479 |         break;

484 |

487 |         break;

490 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
491 |         break;
492 |

495 |         break;
496 | 
497 |       case Token.STRING:
498 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
499 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
500 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
501 |         }
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.GET:
505 |       case Token.SET:
506 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT

521 |         break;

526 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
527 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
528 |         typeable = false;
529 |         break;
530 |

532 |         visitVar(t, n);
533 |         typeable = false;
534 |         break;

539 |         break;

542 |         visitCall(t, n);

544 |         break;

556 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
557 |         break;

585 |         break;

590 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());

593 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
594 |         TernaryValue result =
595 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
596 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
597 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
598 |             result = result.not();
599 |           }

602 |         }

604 |         break;
605 |       }

608 |       case Token.SHNE: {
609 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
610 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
611 | 
612 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
613 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
614 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
615 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
616 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
617 |               rightType.toString());
618 |         }
619 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
620 |         break;
621 |       }
622 | 
623 |       case Token.LT:
624 |       case Token.LE:
625 |       case Token.GT:
626 |       case Token.GE:
627 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
628 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
629 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
630 |           validator.expectNumber(
631 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
632 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
633 |           validator.expectNumber(
634 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
635 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
636 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
637 |           // OK.
638 |         } else {
639 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
640 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
641 |           // should match a string context.
642 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
643 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
644 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
645 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
646 |           message = "right side of comparison";
647 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
648 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
649 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
650 |         }

652 |         break;
653 | 
654 |       case Token.IN:
655 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
656 |         right = n.getLastChild();
657 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
658 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
659 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
660 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
661 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
662 |         break;
663 | 
664 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
665 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
666 |         right = n.getLastChild();
667 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
668 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();

674 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
675 |         break;
676 | 
677 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
678 |         visitAssign(t, n);
679 |         typeable = false;
680 |         break;
681 | 
682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:

709 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
710 |         }

712 |         break;

716 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
717 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
718 |         typeable = false;
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.WITH: {
722 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
723 |         childType = getJSType(child);
724 |         validator.expectObject(
725 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
726 |         typeable = false;
727 |         break;
728 |       }

732 |         break;
733 | 
734 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
735 |       case Token.LABEL:
736 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
737 |       case Token.SWITCH:
738 |       case Token.BREAK:
739 |       case Token.CATCH:
740 |       case Token.TRY:
741 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
742 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
743 |       case Token.BLOCK:
744 |       case Token.EMPTY:
745 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
746 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
747 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
748 |       case Token.THROW:

750 |         break;
751 |

753 |       case Token.DO:

756 |       case Token.WHILE:

785 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
786 |         break;
787 |     }
788 | 
789 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
790 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;

794 |     }

457 |     @Override
458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
460 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
462 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
463 |

473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }
477 |     }

===== M0838 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java

===== M0849 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

Method code:
1063 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1064 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1065 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1066 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1067 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1068 |     if (var != null) {
1069 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1070 |       if (varType != null) {
1071 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1072 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1073 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1074 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1075 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1076 |         } else {
1077 |           return varType;
1078 |         }
1079 |       }
1080 |     }
1081 | 
1082 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1083 |     if (objType != null) {
1084 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1085 |     }
1086 | 
1087 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1088 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1089 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1090 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1091 |       if (regType != null) {
1092 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1093 |       }
1094 |     }
1095 | 
1096 |     return propertyType;
1097 |   }

===== M0860 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }

432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }

Original range: 1365-1519

/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1365 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1366 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1367 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1368 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1369 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1370 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1371 | 
1372 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1373 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1374 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1375 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1376 | 
1377 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1378 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1379 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1380 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1381 |         }
1382 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1383 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1385 | 
1386 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1387 |         return createOptionalType(
1388 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1389 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1390 | 
1391 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1392 |         return createOptionalType(
1393 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1394 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1395 | 
1396 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1397 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1398 | 
1399 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1400 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1401 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1402 | 
1403 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1404 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1405 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1406 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1407 |           builder.addAlternate(
1408 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1409 |         }
1410 |         return builder.build();
1411 | 
1412 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1413 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1414 | 
1415 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1416 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1417 | 
1418 |       case Token.STRING:
1419 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1420 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1421 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1422 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1423 |         }
1424 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1425 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1426 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1427 |           if (typeList != null &&
1428 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1429 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1430 |             JSType parameterType =
1431 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1432 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1433 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1434 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1435 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1436 |               JSType indexType =
1437 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1438 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1439 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1440 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1441 |             }
1442 |           }
1443 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1444 |         } else {
1445 |           return namedType;
1446 |         }
1447 | 
1448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1449 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1450 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1451 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1452 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1453 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1454 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1455 |           thisType =
1456 |               ObjectType.cast(
1457 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1458 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1459 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1460 |           if (thisType == null) {
1461 |             reporter.warning(
1462 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1463 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1464 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1465 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1466 |                 sourceName,
1467 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1468 |           }
1469 | 
1470 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1471 |           current = current.getNext();
1472 |         }
1473 | 
1474 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1475 | 
1476 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1477 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1478 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1479 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1480 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1481 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1482 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1483 |               } else {
1484 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1485 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1486 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1487 |               }
1488 |             } else {
1489 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1490 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1491 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1492 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1493 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1494 |                   reporter.warning(
1495 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1496 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1497 |                 }
1498 |               } else {
1499 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1500 |               }
1501 |             }
1502 |           }
1503 |           current = current.getNext();
1504 |         }
1505 | 
1506 |         JSType returnType =
1507 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1508 | 
1509 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1510 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1511 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1512 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1513 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1514 |             .build();
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1518 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1519 |   }

/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
236 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
237 |       options.checkTypes = true;
238 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
239 |       options.checkTypes = false;
240 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
241 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
242 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
243 |       // parser type warnings.
244 |       options.setWarningLevel(
245 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
246 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
247 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
253 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
257 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
258 |     guards.add(
259 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
260 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
261 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
262 | 
263 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
264 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
265 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
266 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
267 |     // error off.
268 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
269 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
270 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
271 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
272 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
276 |   }
